march to a million: in progress

Archive records: 16 / 1,000,000 target · 16 records (populated from the live archive index)

Every record here is one complete piano song: the full note data for both hands, the form, the key, the tempo and the playing guidance, plus a working demo program that prints the whole song timeline. Nothing is a preview and nothing is truncated: press Play and the whole song sounds, start to finish. Everything is free forever, with no payments and no upsells. No lyrics are included anywhere, because these are instrumental piano songs.

Specialist AI: JAH Jazz Piano Guide

JAH Jazz Piano Guide answers questions about these songs, explains any record in plain sentences, talks through the form and the harmony, and helps a player choose the right tempo and practice path for their level.

Boundless generator

The generator below is seeded and deterministic, throttled to at most 100 records per call, duplicate-checked, and hard-stopped at 10^15 records. Every generated record is labeled src "generated" and is itself a complete song. Open the page script to use window.PianoSongsJazzSignatureTools.generateRecord(seed).

Validation engine

Each record is checked for a complete title, its own record kind, its own edition slug and id prefix, a full prose description, complete-song shape (at least 16 bars, at least 64 notes, at least 30 seconds at tempo, at least 3 sections, both hands present, ten-finger polyphony limit), and a demo whose stored output byte-matches a fresh run.

Evidence links

  • Full record index for this edition (data-2)

Tool connections

The page exposes window.PianoSongsJazzSignatureTools with searchRecords, generateRecord, validateRecord, and runTests.

Testing suite

Run window.PianoSongsJazzSignatureTools.runTests() in the console: it checks record kind, slug, id prefix, src labeling, complete-song validation, duplicate rejection, batch throttling, and search.

Audit history

Built as a package on 2026-10-26: 16 records, ordered simple to compounded to complex by ladder rank, JSONL re-parsed, every demo re-executed with byte-matched output, both generator files syntax-checked, and generator gates executed.

The Copper Kettle Blues Rank 1 · simple · jazz piano · beginner

The Copper Kettle Blues is a complete jazz piano song in D major, in 4/4 time at 96 beats per minute, running 32 bars in A-A-B-A form across 4 sections. Its mood is swing feel and extended harmony over walking or striding bass. The A sections move through ii-V-I-vi turnaround, while the contrasting section changes the harmonic color before the form returns home. This is a Signature-original composition, written new in the genre's language. It is not a copy of any existing song and it is not attributed to any composer outside the Signature system. To play it, keep the left hand steady and unhurried while the right hand sings above it: walking bass in the left hand Practice it first hands separately, then together at half tempo, and only then at full tempo, listening for clean releases between chords so no harmony blurs into the next.

Key: D major
Tempo: 96 BPM
Time: 4/4
Bars: 32
Duration: 80.0 s
Structure: A-A-B-A
Notes: 256 (left 128, right 128)
Composer: Signature original
Provenance: Signature-original composition

Technique: Left hand walks the bass in steady quarter notes through the chord tones. Keep the tempo relaxed and the melody singing; accuracy matters more than speed.

Its mood is swing feel and extended harmony over walking or striding bass. The A sections move through ii-V-I-vi turnaround, while the contrasting section changes the harmonic color before the form returns home. This is a Signature-original composition, written new in the genre's language. It is not a copy of any existing song and it is not attributed to any composer outside the Signature system. To play it, keep the left hand steady and unhurried while the right hand sings above it: walking bass in the left hand Practice it first hands separately, then together at half tempo, and only then at full tempo, listening for clean releases between chords so no harmony blurs into the next.

The Bebop Courtyard is a complete jazz piano song in E major, in 4/4 time at 96 beats per minute, running 32 bars in A-A-B-A form across 4 sections. The All-Night Diner Swing Rank 7 · compounded · jazz piano · intermediate

The All-Night Diner Swing is a complete jazz piano song in G major, in 4/4 time at 64 beats per minute, running 32 bars in A-A-B-A form across 4 sections. Its mood is swing feel and extended harmony over walking or striding bass. The A sections move through ii-V-I-vi turnaround, while the contrasting section changes the harmonic color before the form returns home. This is a Signature-original composition, written new in the genre's language. It is not a copy of any existing song and it is not attributed to any composer outside the Signature system. To play it, keep the left hand steady and unhurried while the right hand sings above it: striding bass and chords in the left hand Practice it first hands separately, then together at half tempo, and only then at full tempo, listening for clean releases between chords so no harmony blurs into the next.

This is a Signature-original composition, written new in the genre's language. It is not a copy of any existing song and it is not attributed to any composer outside the Signature system. Bebop at the Bus Stop is a complete jazz piano song in Bb major, in 4/4 time at 132 beats per minute, running 32 bars in A-A-B-A form across 4 sections. NAMES = ['C', 'C#', 'D', 'D#', 'E', 'F', 'F#', 'G', 'G#', 'A', 'A#', 'B']
def name(m):
    return NAMES[m % 12] + str(m // 12 - 1)
print('Last Set at the Corner Club')
print('Genre: jazz piano | Era: Signature contemporary, jazz language | Key: F major | Time: 4/4 | Tempo: 176 BPM | Bars: 32 | Structure: A-B-A-B')
for start, midi, dur, hand in sorted(NOTES):
    print("beat %.1f: MIDI %d (%s) dur %.1f hand %s" % (start, midi, name(midi), dur, "R" if hand == 1 else "L"))
print("SONG COMPLETE: %d notes, %d beats, %.1f seconds" % (len(NOTES), 128, 128 * 60.0 / 176))
